Evidence before assertions, always. ([xbklairith/kisune](https://github.com/xbklairith/kisune/tree/HEAD/dev-workflow/skills/completion-validation) · ★4)
- **completion-validation**: Reconciles intended feature against blind inference. Categorical alignment (MATCH/PARTIAL/MISMATCH) with critical miss override. No testing — comparison and decision only. Triggers on "validate completion", "reconcile intent vs implementation", "is feature complete", or as final step in feature pipeline. ([autoencoders/skills](https://github.com/autoencoders/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/implement/skills/completion-validation))
- **completion-verification**: Verify all phases are complete with weighted quality scoring before allowing session exit. ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/methodologies/planning-with-files/skills/completion-verification) · ★1.9k)
- **completion-verification**: Verifies a completion, fix, passing, compatibility, release, or readiness claim with fresh task-specific evidence mapped to the actual requirements. Use before saying a change is done, before merge, after review fixes, and at release gates. Not a substitute for diagnosing the cause of a failing check. ([thiientv/godmode](https://github.com/thiientv/godmode/tree/HEAD/skills/completion-verification) · ★101)
- **completion-verification**: Headcount organizational definition-of-done checklist, acceptance verification, and milestone sign-off criteria. ([siddu1004/SFP](https://github.com/siddu1004/SFP/tree/HEAD/plugins/business-ops/skills/completion-verification) · ★5)
- **completion-verification**: Use right before claiming a task is complete, fixed, or passing - runs a self-check gate that requires fresh evidence and a lateral check for the same issue elsewhere, before any success claim. ([rummelonp/dotfiles](https://github.com/rummelonp/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skills/completion-verification) · ★4)
- **completion-verification**: Verify pending done, fixed, or ready claims against supplied acceptance criteria using fresh evidence, explicit coverage, failure classification, and residual-risk reporting. ([blockedby/completion-verification-skill](https://github.com/blockedby/completion-verification-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **completion-verification-gate_anthropic**: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always. Supersedes `verification-before-completion`. ([ildunari/skillshare](https://github.com/ildunari/skillshare/tree/HEAD/skills/coding/completion-verification-gate_anthropic))
- **completion-verifier**: Use before claiming a feature, plugin change, UI, API, backend, or full-stack implementation is complete. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/kevin592/codex-full-stack-workflow/skills/completion-verifier) · ★1.6k)
- **completion-verifier**: Use BEFORE marking any task complete, before user handoffs, or at quality checkpoints — verifies requirements satisfied, edge cases tested, tests pass, no regressions, deliverables ready. Triggers on 'are we done', 'is this complete', 'ready to ship'. NOT for general code review (use copilot) or pre-PR comprehensive review (use /pr-review-toolkit:review-pr). ([dangeles/claude](https://github.com/dangeles/claude/tree/HEAD/claude-config/skills/completion-verifier) · ★7 · 3 copies)
- **completion-verifier**: Run required checks and decide whether implementation has enough evidence to be treated as complete. ([munlucky/moonshot-relay](https://github.com/munlucky/moonshot-relay/tree/HEAD/skills/completion-verifier))
- **completion-verifier**: Use when a workflow stage is about to be marked complete and outputs, document state, or command-backed evidence must be checked first, including 完成前核对/交付证据校验/阶段完成验收 ([yxzyzh08/dev-workflow-skills](https://github.com/yxzyzh08/dev-workflow-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/completion-verifier))
- **completion-verify**: Verify a session's own work (build, types, lint, tests, diff scope, quick security check) before claiming a task is done. Use when about to say "done", before opening a PR, or after a feature/refactor is finished. ([manastalukdar/ai-devstudio](https://github.com/manastalukdar/ai-devstudio/tree/HEAD/skills/completion-verify) · ★1)
- **completion-vs-acceptance**: Analyze the gap between an agent's self-reported completion rate and the actual acceptance rate (tasks the user or downstream validator kept without correction). Produces a 2x2 quadrant — Trusted, Overconfident, Conservative, Failing — for each workflow type. Use when the user says "completion rate", "acceptance rate", "how trusted is my agent", "agent done vs actually accepted", "completion-vs-acceptance", or wants to understand which agent workflows have earned autonomy. ([m2ai-portfolio/m2ai-skills-pack](https://github.com/m2ai-portfolio/m2ai-skills-pack/tree/HEAD/m2ai-agent-ops/skills/completion-vs-acceptance) · ★4)
- **completion_proof**: Local report-only detector for completion claims without evidence markers (A11). Does not authenticate evidence or block builds. ([zedarvates/botte-secrete](https://github.com/zedarvates/botte-secrete/tree/HEAD/skills/completion_proof) · ★2)

- **completitud-cobertura**: Skill FULL del módulo REFLEJO STATELESS `completitud-cobertura` de la vertical contabilidad (Enki). PRODUCE LA MÉTRICA ÚNICA DE COBERTURA que Q3/C6/P4 LEEN (no la recalculan): cobertura = min(recibidos, esperados) / esperados, acotada a [0,1], determinista y sin ponderaciones ocultas. Escucha `contabilidad.hecho_recibido` y guarda una ventana acotada en memoria para medir cuando no se declaran los totales. Honestidad (invariante 13): sin dato no inventa el porcentaje — `cobertura:null` y `abierto` declarado. No persiste (STATELESS). La op `medir` es PREGUNTA → sin ui_handler. ([Noninapizzicas/3enki](https://github.com/Noninapizzicas/3enki/tree/HEAD/modules/cosecha/cantera/enki/completitud-cobertura))
- **completor**: UI/UX design review for screenshots, live URLs, Figma frames, or source code — four modes. Use when someone shares a design and wants feedback: "review my dashboard," "audit this against forms," "roast my landing page," "what's broken here," "does this look right," "is this accessible," "check the contrast," "what's missing," "critique my onboarding," "compare these two," "fix what you flagged," "does this pass WCAG," "review my checkout," "is this component done." Covers websites, web apps, mobile apps, design system components, and user flows. Four modes — audit (item-by-item check against… ([amandeepintl/Completor](https://github.com/amandeepintl/Completor/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **complex**: Complex Numbers quiz covering addition, multiplication, modulus (absolute value), and squaring. Teaches students to: - Add and subtract complex numbers (separate real and imaginary parts) - Multiply complex numbers using FOIL - Calculate the modulus |z| = √(a² + b²) for z = a + bi - Square complex numbers using the expansion (a+bi)² = a² - b² + 2abi ([vicharanashala/tenali](https://github.com/vicharanashala/tenali/tree/HEAD/supermarket/complex) · ★20 · 3 copies)
